    name: fitnep
    description: "FitNEP nutrition and fitness app for Android and iOS."
    publish_to: 'none'
    version: 1.0.0+1
    
    environment:
      sdk: ^3.13.4
    
    dependencies:
      flutter:
        sdk: flutter
    
      cupertino_icons: ^1.0.8
      flutter_riverpod: ^3.5.0
      go_router: ^18.1.4
      dio: ^5.11.1
      flutter_secure_storage: ^11.2.0
      intl: ^0.20.3
      firebase_core: ^5.2.0
      firebase_auth: ^7.1.0
      cloud_firestore: ^7.2.0
      cloud_functions: ^7.1.0
      google_fonts: ^6.2.1
    
    dev_dependencies:
      flutter_test:
        sdk: flutter
      integration_test:
        sdk: flutter
    
      flutter_lints: ^6.0.0
    
    dependency_overrides:
      # 2.3.x uses JNI/CMake and currently hits native metadata file locks on Windows.
      path_provider_android: 2.2.23
    
    flutter:
      uses-material-design: true
    
      # Enable asset directories once real files are added:
      # assets:
      #   - assets/images/
      #   - assets/icons/
      #   - assets/animations/
    
      # Declare font families here after adding font files to assets/fonts/.
    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    
    import 'app/app.dart';
    
    void main() {
      WidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
      runApp(const ProviderScope(child: FitNepApp()));
    }
    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    
    import '../core/services/firebase_providers.dart';
    import '../features/authentication/presentation/password_reset_session_observer.dart';
    import '../shared/widgets/app_startup_view.dart';
    import 'routes/app_router.dart';
    import 'theme/app_theme.dart';
    
    class FitNepApp extends ConsumerWidget {
      const FitNepApp({super.key});
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
        final initialization = ref.watch(firebaseInitializationProvider);
    
        return MaterialApp.router(
          title: 'FitNEP',
          debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
          theme: AppTheme.light,
          darkTheme: AppTheme.dark,
          routerConfig: ref.watch(appRouterProvider),
          builder: (context, child) => initialization.when(
            data: (_) => PasswordResetSessionObserver(
              child: child ?? const SizedBox.shrink(),
            ),
            loading: () => const AppStartupView(),
            error: (error, stackTrace) => AppStartupView(
              onRetry: () => ref.invalidate(firebaseInitializationProvider),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    }
    abstract final class AppRoutes {
      static const welcome = 'welcome';
      static const about = 'about';
      static const login = 'login';
      static const forgotPassword = 'forgotPassword';
      static const resetPassword = 'resetPassword';
    
      static const welcomePath = '/';
      static const aboutPath = '/about';
      static const loginPath = '/login';
      static const forgotPasswordPath = '/forgot-password';
      static const resetPasswordPath = '/reset-password';
    
      // Shared
      static const notifications = '/notifications';
      static const dietPlanDetails = '/diet-plans/:id';
      static const messageThread = '/messages/:id';
      static const taskDetails = '/tasks/:id';
      static const paymentDetails = '/payments/:id';
    
      // Client
      static const clientHome = '/client/home';
      static const clientNotifications = '/client/notifications';
    
      static const dietPlanDetailsName = 'diet-plan-details';
      static const messageThreadName = 'message-thread';
      static const taskDetailsName = 'task-details';
      static const paymentDetailsName = 'payment-details';
      static const clientHomeName = 'client-home';
      static const clientNotificationsName = 'client-notifications';
    
      // Admin
      static const adminClients = '/admin/clients';
      static const clientProfile = '/admin/clients/:id';
      static const auditLog = '/admin/audit-log';
    }
    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
    
    import '../../features/authentication/presentation/pages/password_recovery_pages.dart';
    import '../../features/authentication/presentation/pages/login_page.dart';
    import '../../features/onboarding/presentation/pages/about_page.dart';
    import '../../features/onboarding/presentation/pages/welcome_page.dart';
    import '../../shared/widgets/app_page.dart';
    import '../../features/admin/presentation/pages/client_list_page.dart';
    import '../../features/admin/presentation/pages/client_profile_page.dart';
    import '../../features/admin/presentation/pages/audit_log_page.dart';
    import '../../features/dashboards/presentation/pages/client_home_page.dart';
    import '../../features/notifications/presentation/pages/notifications_page.dart';
    import '../../features/notifications/domain/entities/notification_entity.dart';
    import '../../features/notifications/presentation/pages/notification_destination_page.dart';
    import 'app_routes.dart';
    
    final appRouterProvider = Provider<GoRouter>((ref) {
      final router = GoRouter(
        initialLocation: AppRoutes.welcomePath,
        routes: [
          GoRoute(
            name: AppRoutes.welcome,
            path: AppRoutes.welcomePath,
            builder: (context, state) => const WelcomePage(),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            name: AppRoutes.about,
            path: AppRoutes.aboutPath,
            builder: (context, state) => const AboutPage(),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            name: AppRoutes.login,
            path: AppRoutes.loginPath,
            builder: (context, state) => const LoginPage(),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            name: AppRoutes.forgotPassword,
            path: AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath,
            builder: (context, state) => const ForgotPasswordPage(),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            name: AppRoutes.resetPassword,
            path: AppRoutes.resetPasswordPath,
            builder: (context, state) => ResetPasswordPage(
              key: ValueKey(state.uri.toString()),
              // Preserve the mode/duplicate parameters for service validation.
              initialLink: state.uri.query.isEmpty
                  ? null
                  : 'fitnep://auth/reset-password?${state.uri.query}',
            ),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            path: AppRoutes.clientHome,
            name: AppRoutes.clientHomeName,
            builder: (context, state) => const ClientHomePage(),
          ),
          GoRoute(
            path: AppRoutes.clientNotifications,
            name: AppRoutes.clientNotificationsName,

    class TokenStorage {
      static const _accessTokenKey = 'access_token';
    
      final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;
      final FirebaseAuth? auth;
    
      const TokenStorage({this.auth}) : _storage = const FlutterSecureStorage();
    
      Future<String?> readAccessToken() async {
        final currentAuth = auth;
        if (currentAuth != null) return currentAuth.currentUser?.getIdToken();
        return _storage.read(key: _accessTokenKey);
      }
    
      Future<void> writeAccessToken(String token) =>
          _storage.write(key: _accessTokenKey, value: token);
    
      Future<void> deleteAccessToken() => _storage.delete(key: _accessTokenKey);
    }
    import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
    
    import '../config/app_config.dart';

    class ApiClient {
      final Dio dio;
    
      ApiClient({required TokenStorage tokenStorage, Dio? dio})
        : dio =
              dio ??
              Dio(
                BaseOptions(
                  baseUrl: AppConfig.apiBaseUrl,
                  connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
                  receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
                  headers: const {'Accept': 'application/json'},
                ),
              ) {
        this.dio.interceptors.add(
          InterceptorsWrapper(
            onRequest: (options, handler) async {
              try {
                final token = await tokenStorage.readAccessToken();
                if (token != null && token.trim().isNotEmpty) {
                  options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ${token.trim()}';
                }
                handler.next(options);
              } catch (error) {
                handler.reject(DioException(requestOptions: options, error: error));
              }
            },
          ),
        );
      }
    }

        defaultValue: '',
      );
    
      static String get apiBaseUrl {
        final value = _configuredApiBaseUrl.trim();
        if (value.isEmpty || value.endsWith('/')) return value;
        return '$value/';
      }
    
      static bool get hasApiBaseUrl => apiBaseUrl.isNotEmpty;
    }

    class RecoveryFailure implements Exception {
      const RecoveryFailure(
        this.message, {
        this.invalidLink = false,
        this.passwordUpdated = false,
      });
    
      final String message;
      final bool invalidLink;
      final bool passwordUpdated;
    }
    
    class PasswordRecoveryService {
      PasswordRecoveryService(this.auth);
    
      final FirebaseAuth auth;
    
      static const requestConfirmation =
          'If an account uses this email, you will receive a password reset link. '
          'Check your inbox and spam folder.';
    
      static String? emailError(String? value) {
        final email = value?.trim() ?? '';
        if (email.isEmpty) return 'Enter your registered email address.';
        if (email.length > 254 ||
            !RegExp(r'^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$').hasMatch(email)) {
          return 'Enter a valid email address.';
        }
        return null;
      }
    
      // Accept an email action link or its code; Firebase remains the authority
      // for the code's project, expiry and single-use validity.
      static String actionCode(String value) {
        final input = value.trim();
        final uri = Uri.tryParse(input);
        String? code;
        if (uri != null && uri.hasScheme) {
          final mode = uri.queryParametersAll['mode'];
          final codes = uri.queryParametersAll['oobCode'];
          if ((uri.scheme != 'https' && uri.scheme != 'fitnep') ||
              mode?.length != 1 ||
              mode!.single != 'resetPassword' ||
              codes?.length != 1) {
            throw const RecoveryFailure(
              'Use the password reset link from your email.',
              invalidLink: true,
            );
          }
          code = codes!.single;
        } else {
          code = input;
        }
        if (!RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,2048}$').hasMatch(code)) {
          throw const RecoveryFailure(
            'This reset link is invalid. Request a new link.',
            invalidLink: true,
          );
        }
        return code;
      }
    
      Future<void> request(String email) async {
        final error = emailError(email);
        if (error != null) throw RecoveryFailure(error);
        try {
          await auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email: email.trim());
        } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
          // Older projects can still return these without enumeration protection.
          if (error.code == 'user-not-found' || error.code == 'user-disabled') {
            return;
          }
          throw failureFor(error);
        }
      }
    
      Future<String> verify(String code) async {
        try {
          return await auth.verifyPasswordResetCode(actionCode(code));
        } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
          throw failureFor(error);
        }
      }
    
      Future<void> confirm(String code, String password) async {
        if (password.isEmpty) {
          throw const RecoveryFailure('Enter your new password.');
        }
        try {
          final status = await auth.validatePassword(auth, password);
          if (!status.isValid) {
            final missing = <String>[
              if (!status.meetsMinPasswordLength) 'more characters',
              if (!status.meetsMaxPasswordLength) 'fewer characters',
              if (!status.meetsLowercaseRequirement) 'a lowercase letter',
              if (!status.meetsUppercaseRequirement) 'an uppercase letter',
              if (!status.meetsDigitsRequirement) 'a number',
              if (!status.meetsSymbolsRequirement) 'a special character',
            ];
            throw RecoveryFailure(
              missing.isEmpty
                  ? 'Your password does not meet the account security requirements.'
                  : 'Your password needs ${missing.join(', ')}.',
            );
          }
          await auth.confirmPasswordReset(
            code: actionCode(code),
            newPassword: password,
          );
        } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
          throw failureFor(error);
        } on RecoveryFailure {
          rethrow;
        } catch (_) {
          throw const RecoveryFailure(
            'We couldnâ€™t check your password requirements. Check your connection '
            'and try again.',
          );
        }
        await clearLocalSession();
      }
    
      Future<void> clearLocalSession() async {
        try {
          await auth.signOut();
        } catch (_) {
          // The reset code has already been consumed. Retry sign-out, not reset.
          throw const RecoveryFailure(
            'Your password was updated, but we couldnâ€™t sign out this device. '
            'Please finish signing out before continuing.',
            passwordUpdated: true,
          );
        }
      }
    
      // Call from the shared session lifecycle on resume/refresh. This reports
      // whether an old session ended; it is not authorization for protected data.
      Future<bool> signOutIfSessionRevoked() async {
        final user = auth.currentUser;
        if (user == null) return false;
        final refreshToken = user.refreshToken;
        try {
          await user.getIdToken(true);
          return false;
        } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
          if (!const {
            'user-token-expired',
            'invalid-user-token',
            'user-disabled',
            'user-not-found',
          }.contains(error.code)) {
            rethrow;
          }
          // Preserve a fresh sign-in made while this older session was checked.
          final current = auth.currentUser;
          if (current?.uid == user.uid && current?.refreshToken == refreshToken) {
            await auth.signOut();
          }
          return true;
        }
      }
    
      static RecoveryFailure failureFor(FirebaseAuthException error) {
        return switch (error.code) {
          'expired-action-code' ||
          'invalid-action-code' ||
          'user-disabled' ||
          'user-not-found' => const RecoveryFailure(
            'This reset link has expired or was already used. Request a new link.',
            invalidLink: true,
          ),
          'weak-password' ||
          'password-does-not-meet-requirements' => const RecoveryFailure(
            'Choose a stronger password that meets your account requirements.',
          ),
          'too-many-requests' => const RecoveryFailure(
            'Too many attempts. Please wait a while before trying again.',
          ),
          'network-request-failed' => const RecoveryFailure(
            'Check your connection and try again.',
          ),
          'invalid-email' => const RecoveryFailure('Enter a valid email address.'),
          _ => const RecoveryFailure(
            'Password recovery is unavailable right now. Please try again later.',
          ),
        };
      }
    }

    // US 2 session revocation. Firebase remains the shared authentication state.
    class PasswordResetSessionObserver extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
      const PasswordResetSessionObserver({super.key, required this.child});
      final Widget child;
    
      @override
      ConsumerState<PasswordResetSessionObserver> createState() =>
          _PasswordResetSessionObserverState();
    }
    
    class _PasswordResetSessionObserverState
        extends ConsumerState<PasswordResetSessionObserver>
        with WidgetsBindingObserver {
      late final PasswordRecoveryService _recovery;
      StreamSubscription<User?>? _subscription;
      Timer? _timer;
      bool _checking = false;
    
      @override
      void initState() {
        super.initState();
        _recovery = PasswordRecoveryService(ref.read(firebaseAuthProvider));
        WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
        _subscription = _recovery.auth.authStateChanges().listen((_) {
          ref.invalidate(notificationControllerProvider);
          ref.invalidate(notificationDestinationProvider);
          _startChecking();
        }, onError: (Object error) {});
        _startChecking();
      }
    
      void _startChecking() {
        _timer?.cancel();
        final state = WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState;
        if (!mounted ||
            _recovery.auth.currentUser == null ||
            (state != null && state != AppLifecycleState.resumed)) {
          return;
        }
        _timer = Timer.periodic(
          const Duration(seconds: 30),
          (_) => unawaited(_check()),
        );
        unawaited(_check());
      }
    
      Future<void> _check() async {
        if (_checking || !mounted) return;
        _checking = true;
        try {
          final ended = await _recovery.signOutIfSessionRevoked();
          if (mounted && ended && _recovery.auth.currentUser == null) {
            _timer?.cancel();
            final router = ref.read(appRouterProvider);
            final path = router.routeInformationProvider.value.uri.path;
            // Recovery stays usable while signed out; preserve reset confirmation.
            if (path != AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath &&
                path != AppRoutes.resetPasswordPath) {
              router.goNamed(AppRoutes.welcome);
            }
          }
        } catch (_) {
          // Connection failures do not prove revocation. Retry on resume/next tick;
          // protected server operations still require revocation-aware verification.
        } finally {
          _checking = false;
        }
      }
    
      @override
      void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
        if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
          _startChecking();
        } else {
          _timer?.cancel();
        }
      }
    
      @override
      void dispose() {
        _timer?.cancel();
        unawaited(_subscription?.cancel());
        WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
        super.dispose();
      }
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) => widget.child;
    }

    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
    
    import '../../../../app/routes/app_routes.dart';
    import '../../../../app/theme/app_theme.dart';
    import '../../data/password_recovery_service.dart';
    import 'password_recovery_pages.dart';
    
    class LoginPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
      const LoginPage({super.key});
    
      @override
      ConsumerState<LoginPage> createState() => _LoginPageState();
    }
    
    class _LoginPageState extends ConsumerState<LoginPage> {
      final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
      final _emailController = TextEditingController();
      final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
      bool _passwordHidden = true;
      bool _rememberMe = false;
    
      @override
      void dispose() {
        _emailController.dispose();
        _passwordController.dispose();
        super.dispose();
      }
    
      void _submit() {
        FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
        if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    
        _showUnavailable(
          'Secure email and password login has not been connected yet.',
        );
      }
    
      void _showUnavailable(String message) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          ..hideCurrentSnackBar()
          ..showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
      }
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) {
        return RecoveryPage(
          title: 'Welcome back',
          description: 'Your Health Is Your Biggest Asset. Personalized nutrition and fitness support, built around you.',
          child: AutofillGroup(
            child: Form(
              key: _formKey,
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'YOUR WELLNESS, ELEVATED.',
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelMedium?.copyWith(
                      color: AppColors.primary,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      letterSpacing: 1.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _emailController,
                    keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                    autofillHints: const [
                      AutofillHints.username,
                      AutofillHints.email,
                    ],
                    autocorrect: false,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Email address',
                      hintText: 'you@example.com',
                      prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.mail_outline),
                    ),
                    validator: PasswordRecoveryService.emailError,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextFormField(
                    controller: _passwordController,
                    obscureText: _passwordHidden,
                    autocorrect: false,
                    enableSuggestions: false,
                    textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                    autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.password],
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Password',
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
                      suffixIcon: IconButton(
                        tooltip: _passwordHidden
                            ? 'Show password'
                            : 'Hide password',
                        onPressed: () =>
                            setState(() => _passwordHidden = !_passwordHidden),
                        icon: Icon(
                          _passwordHidden
                              ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                              : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    validator: (value) => value == null || value.isEmpty
                        ? 'Enter your password.'
                        : null,
                    onFieldSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: InkWell(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          onTap: () => setState(() => _rememberMe = !_rememberMe),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              Checkbox(
                                value: _rememberMe,
                                onChanged: (value) =>
                                    setState(() => _rememberMe = value ?? false),
                              ),
                              const Flexible(child: Text('Remember me')),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed: () =>
                            context.pushNamed(AppRoutes.forgotPassword),
                        child: const Text('Forgot password?'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  FilledButton(onPressed: _submit, child: const Text('Log in')),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      const Flexible(child: Text("Don't have an account?")),
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed: () => _showUnavailable(
                          'Account registration is not available yet.',
                        ),
                        child: const Text('Sign up'),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    class RecoveryMessage extends StatelessWidget {
      const RecoveryMessage(this.message, {super.key});
      final String message;
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) => Semantics(
        liveRegion: true,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 16),

            message,

    class ForgotPasswordPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
      const ForgotPasswordPage({super.key});
    
      @override
      ConsumerState<ForgotPasswordPage> createState() => _ForgotPasswordPageState();
    }
    
    class _ForgotPasswordPageState extends ConsumerState<ForgotPasswordPage> {
      final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
      final _email = TextEditingController();
      bool _busy = false;
      bool _sent = false;
      String? _error;
    
      @override
      void dispose() {
        _email.dispose();
        super.dispose();
      }
    
      Future<void> _send() async {
        if (_busy || !_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
        FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
        setState(() {
          _busy = true;
          _error = null;
        });
        try {
          await ref.read(passwordRecoveryProvider).request(_email.text);
          if (mounted) setState(() => _sent = true);
        } on RecoveryFailure catch (error) {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _error = error.message);
        } catch (_) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(
              () => _error = 'We couldnâ€™t send your request. Please try again.',
            );
          }
        } finally {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
        }
      }
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) => RecoveryPage(
        title: _sent ? 'Check your email' : 'Forgot password?',
        description: _sent ? PasswordRecoveryService.requestConfirmation : 'Enter your registered email address to get a secure password reset link.',
        child: _sent
            ? Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  FilledButton(
                    onPressed: () => context.pushNamed(AppRoutes.resetPassword),
                    child: const Text('Use a reset link'),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() {
                      _sent = false;
                      _email.clear();
                    }),
                    child: const Text('Try another email'),
                  ),
                ],
              )
            : Form(
                key: _form,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    if (_error != null) RecoveryMessage(_error!),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _email,
                      readOnly: _busy,
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                      autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.email],
                      autocorrect: false,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Email address',
                        hintText: 'you@example.com',
                        prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.mail_outline),
                      ),
                      validator: PasswordRecoveryService.emailError,
                      onFieldSubmitted: (_) => _send(),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    FilledButton(
                      onPressed: _busy ? null : _send,
                      child: Text(_busy ? 'Sendingâ€¦' : 'Send reset link'),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: _busy
                          ? null
                          : () => context.pushNamed(AppRoutes.resetPassword),
                      child: const Text('Already have a reset link?'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
      );
    }
    
    class ResetPasswordPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
      const ResetPasswordPage({super.key, this.initialLink});
      final String? initialLink;
    
      @override
      ConsumerState<ResetPasswordPage> createState() => _ResetPasswordPageState();
    }
    
    class _ResetPasswordPageState extends ConsumerState<ResetPasswordPage> {
      final _form = GlobalKey<FormState>();
      final _link = TextEditingController();
      final _password = TextEditingController();
      final _confirmation = TextEditingController();
      String? _code;
      String? _error;
      bool _busy = false;
      bool _invalidLink = false;
      bool _complete = false;
      bool _needsSignOut = false;
    
      @override
      void initState() {
        super.initState();
        final link = widget.initialLink;
        if (link != null) {
          _link.text = link;
          Future.microtask(_verify);
        }
      }
    
      @override
      void dispose() {
        _link.dispose();
        _password.dispose();
        _confirmation.dispose();
        super.dispose();
      }
    
      Future<void> _verify() async {
        if (!mounted || _busy) return;
        FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
        setState(() {
          _busy = true;
          _error = null;
          _invalidLink = false;
        });
        try {
          final code = PasswordRecoveryService.actionCode(_link.text);
          await ref.read(passwordRecoveryProvider).verify(code);
          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _code = code;
              _link.clear();
            });
          }
        } on RecoveryFailure catch (error) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _error = error.message;
              _invalidLink = error.invalidLink;
            });
          }
        } catch (_) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(
              () => _error = 'We couldnâ€™t verify your link. Check your connection and try again.',
            );
          }
        } finally {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
        }
      }
    
      Future<void> _reset() async {
        if (_busy || !_form.currentState!.validate()) return;
        FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
        setState(() {
          _busy = true;
          _error = null;
        });
        try {
          await ref.read(passwordRecoveryProvider).confirm(_code!, _password.text);
          if (!mounted) return;
          _clearSecrets();
          if (mounted) setState(() => _complete = true);
        } on RecoveryFailure catch (error) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(() {
              _error = error.message;
              _invalidLink = error.invalidLink;
              if (error.passwordUpdated) {
                _clearSecrets();
                _complete = true;
                _needsSignOut = true;
              }
            });
          }
        } catch (_) {
          if (mounted) {
            setState(
              () => _error = 'We couldnâ€™t reset your password. Please try again.',
            );
          }
        } finally {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
        }
      }
    
      void _clearSecrets() {
        _password.clear();
        _confirmation.clear();
        _link.clear();
        _code = null;
      }
    
      Future<void> _finishSignOut() async {
        if (_busy) return;
        setState(() {
          _busy = true;
          _error = null;
        });
        try {
          await ref.read(passwordRecoveryProvider).clearLocalSession();
          if (mounted) setState(() => _needsSignOut = false);
        } on RecoveryFailure catch (error) {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _error = error.message);
        } finally {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
        }
      }
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) => RecoveryPage(
        title: _complete
            ? 'Password updated'
            : _code == null
            ? 'Use your reset link'
            : 'Create a new password',
        description: _complete
            ? 'Sign in again with your new password.'
            : _code == null
            ? 'Copy the password reset link from your email and paste it below.'
            : 'Choose a unique password that meets your account security requirements.',
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            if (_error != null) RecoveryMessage(_error!),
            if (_complete)
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: _busy
                    ? null
                    : _needsSignOut
                    ? _finishSignOut
                    : () => context.goNamed(AppRoutes.welcome),
                child: Text(
                  _busy
                      ? 'Signing outâ€¦'
                      : _needsSignOut
                      ? 'Finish signing out'
                      : 'Back to FitNEP',
                ),
              )
            else if (_invalidLink)
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: () => context.goNamed(AppRoutes.forgotPassword),
                child: const Text('Request a new reset link'),
              )
            else if (_code == null) ...[
              TextField(
                controller: _link,
                readOnly: _busy,
                autocorrect: false,
                enableSuggestions: false,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.url,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  labelText: 'Reset link or code',
                  prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.link),
                ),
                onSubmitted: (_) => _verify(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: _busy ? null : _verify,
                child: Text(_busy ? 'Checkingâ€¦' : 'Continue'),
              ),
            ] else
              Form(
                key: _form,
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                  children: [
                    RecoveryPasswordField(
                      controller: _password,
                      label: 'New password',
                      enabled: !_busy,
                      validator: (value) => value == null || value.isEmpty
                          ? 'Enter your new password.'
                          : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    RecoveryPasswordField(
                      controller: _confirmation,
                      label: 'Confirm new password',
                      enabled: !_busy,
                      validator: (value) => value != _password.text
                          ? 'Your passwords do not match.'
                          : null,
                      onSubmitted: (_) => _reset(),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    FilledButton(
                      onPressed: _busy ? null : _reset,
                      child: Text(_busy ? 'Updatingâ€¦' : 'Update password'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }
    
    class RecoveryPasswordField extends StatefulWidget {
      const RecoveryPasswordField({
        super.key,
        required this.controller,
        required this.label,
        required this.validator,
        required this.enabled,
        this.onSubmitted,
      });
      final TextEditingController controller;
      final String label;
      final FormFieldValidator<String> validator;
      final bool enabled;
      final ValueChanged<String>? onSubmitted;
    
      @override
      State<RecoveryPasswordField> createState() => _RecoveryPasswordFieldState();
    }
    
    class _RecoveryPasswordFieldState extends State<RecoveryPasswordField> {
      bool _hidden = true;
    
      @override
      Widget build(BuildContext context) => TextFormField(
        controller: widget.controller,
        readOnly: !widget.enabled,
        obscureText: _hidden,
        autocorrect: false,
        enableSuggestions: false,
        textInputAction: widget.onSubmitted == null
            ? TextInputAction.next
            : TextInputAction.done,
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          labelText: widget.label,
          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.lock_outline),
          suffixIcon: IconButton(
            tooltip: _hidden
                ? 'Show ${widget.label.toLowerCase()}'
                : 'Hide ${widget.label.toLowerCase()}',
            onPressed: widget.enabled
                ? () => setState(() => _hidden = !_hidden)
                : null,
            icon: Icon(
              _hidden ? Icons.visibility_outlined : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
            ),
          ),
        ),
        validator: widget.validator,
        onFieldSubmitted: widget.onSubmitted,
      );
    }
    import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
    
    class SignedOutAuth extends Fake implements FirebaseAuth {
      @override
      User? get currentUser => null;
      @override
      Stream<User?> authStateChanges() => Stream<User?>.value(null);
    }
    import 'dart:async';
    
    import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/app.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/routes/app_router.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/routes/app_routes.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/core/services/firebase_providers.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/features/authentication/data/password_recovery_service.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/features/authentication/presentation/pages/password_recovery_pages.dart';
    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
    
    import '../support/signed_out_auth.dart';
    
    class _UnusedAuth extends Fake implements FirebaseAuth {}
    
    class _Recovery extends PasswordRecoveryService {
      _Recovery() : super(_UnusedAuth());
      RecoveryFailure? error;
      Completer<void>? pending;
      int requests = 0;
      int resets = 0;
      int signOuts = 0;
      String? password;
    
      @override
      Future<void> request(String email) async {
        requests++;
        if (pending != null) await pending!.future;
        if (error != null) throw error!;
      }
    
      @override
      Future<String> verify(String code) async {
        if (error != null) throw error!;
        return 'client@example.com';
      }
    
      @override
      Future<void> confirm(String code, String newPassword) async {
        resets++;
        if (pending != null) await pending!.future;
        if (error != null) throw error!;
        password = newPassword;
      }
    
      @override
      Future<void> clearLocalSession() async {
        signOuts++;
      }
    }
    
    Future<ProviderContainer> _open(
      WidgetTester tester,
      _Recovery recovery,
      String route,
    ) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(
        ProviderScope(
          overrides: [
            firebaseAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(SignedOutAuth()),
            firebaseInitializationProvider.overrideWith((ref) async {}),
            passwordRecoveryProvider.overrideWithValue(recovery),
          ],
          child: const FitNepApp(),
        ),
      );
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      final container = ProviderScope.containerOf(
        tester.element(find.byType(FitNepApp)),
      );
      container.read(appRouterProvider).go(route);
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      return container;
    }
    
    void main() {
      testWidgets('cold recovery link opens after Firebase becomes ready', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        tester.binding.platformDispatcher.defaultRouteNameTestValue =
            'fitnep://auth/forgot-password';
        addTearDown(
          tester.binding.platformDispatcher.clearDefaultRouteNameTestValue,
        );
        final ready = Completer<void>();
        await tester.pumpWidget(
          ProviderScope(
            overrides: [
              firebaseAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(SignedOutAuth()),
              firebaseInitializationProvider.overrideWith((ref) => ready.future),
            ],
            child: const FitNepApp(),
          ),
        );
        await tester.pump();
        expect(find.text('Send reset link'), findsNothing);
        ready.complete();
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text('Send reset link'), findsOneWidget);
      });
      testWidgets(
        'cleanup failure retries sign-out without replaying successful reset',
        (tester) async {
          final recovery = _Recovery();
          await _open(
            tester,
            recovery,
            '/reset-password?mode=resetPassword&oobCode=valid_code',
          );
          recovery.error = const RecoveryFailure(
            'Finish signing out.',
            passwordUpdated: true,
          );
          await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField).at(0), 'Stronger!12');
          await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField).at(1), 'Stronger!12');
          await tester.tap(find.text('Update password'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          expect(find.text('Password updated'), findsOneWidget);
          expect(find.byType(TextFormField), findsNothing);
          await tester.tap(find.text('Finish signing out'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          expect(recovery.resets, 1);
          expect(recovery.signOuts, 1);
          expect(find.text('Back to FitNEP'), findsOneWidget);
        },
      );
      testWidgets('recovery routes remain gated on Firebase initialization', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final ready = Completer<void>();
        await tester.pumpWidget(
          ProviderScope(
            overrides: [
              firebaseAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(SignedOutAuth()),
              firebaseInitializationProvider.overrideWith((ref) => ready.future),
            ],
            child: const FitNepApp(),
          ),
        );
        final container = ProviderScope.containerOf(
          tester.element(find.byType(FitNepApp)),
        );
        container.read(appRouterProvider).go(AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath);
        await tester.pump();
        expect(find.text('Send reset link'), findsNothing);
        ready.complete();
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text('Send reset link'), findsOneWidget);
      });
    
      testWidgets('invalid email blocks submission and repeated taps send once', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final recovery = _Recovery()..pending = Completer<void>();
        await _open(tester, recovery, AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath);
        await tester.tap(find.text('Send reset link'));
        await tester.pump();
        expect(recovery.requests, 0);
        await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField), 'client@example.com');
        await tester.tap(find.text('Send reset link'));
        await tester.pump();
        await tester.tap(find.text('Sendingâ€¦'));
        expect(recovery.requests, 1);
        recovery.pending!.complete();
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(
          find.text(PasswordRecoveryService.requestConfirmation),
          findsOneWidget,
        );
      });
    
      testWidgets('delivery failure shows retry and successful retry confirms', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final recovery = _Recovery()
          ..error = const RecoveryFailure('Check your connection and try again.');
        await _open(tester, recovery, AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath);
        await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField), 'client@example.com');
        await tester.tap(find.text('Send reset link'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text('Check your connection and try again.'), findsOneWidget);
        recovery.error = null;
        await tester.tap(find.text('Send reset link'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(find.text('Check your email'), findsOneWidget);
      });
    
      testWidgets(
        'reset validates matching passwords, clears secrets, and returns to FitNEP',
        (tester) async {
          final recovery = _Recovery();
          await _open(
            tester,
            recovery,
            '/reset-password?mode=resetPassword&oobCode=valid_code',
          );
          final fields = find.byType(TextFormField);
          await tester.enterText(fields.at(0), '  Stronger!12  ');
          await tester.enterText(fields.at(1), 'wrong');
          await tester.tap(find.text('Update password'));
          await tester.pump();
          expect(recovery.resets, 0);
          expect(find.text('Your passwords do not match.'), findsOneWidget);
          await tester.enterText(fields.at(1), '  Stronger!12  ');
          await tester.tap(find.text('Update password'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          expect(recovery.password, '  Stronger!12  ');
          expect(find.text('Password updated'), findsOneWidget);
          expect(find.byType(TextFormField), findsNothing);
          await tester.tap(find.text('Back to FitNEP'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();
          expect(find.text('Learn about FitNEP'), findsOneWidget);
        },
      );
    
      testWidgets('expired link offers a fresh request and no password form', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final recovery = _Recovery()
          ..error = const RecoveryFailure('Link expired.', invalidLink: true);
        await _open(
          tester,
          recovery,
          '/reset-password?mode=resetPassword&oobCode=valid_code',
        );
        expect(find.byType(RecoveryPasswordField), findsNothing);

      testWidgets('password visibility resets after leaving and returning', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final container = await _open(
          tester,
          _Recovery(),
          '/reset-password?mode=resetPassword&oobCode=valid_code',
        );
        await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Show new password'));
        await tester.pump();
        expect(
          tester.widget<TextField>(find.byType(TextField).first).obscureText,
          isFalse,
        );
        container.read(appRouterProvider).go(AppRoutes.forgotPasswordPath);
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        container
            .read(appRouterProvider)
            .go('/reset-password?mode=resetPassword&oobCode=valid_code');
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        expect(
          tester.widget<TextField>(find.byType(TextField).first).obscureText,
          isTrue,
        );
      });
    
      testWidgets(

          addTearDown(tester.view.resetPhysicalSize);
          addTearDown(tester.view.resetDevicePixelRatio);
          addTearDown(tester.view.resetViewInsets);
          addTearDown(tester.platformDispatcher.clearTextScaleFactorTestValue);

          await tester.ensureVisible(find.text('Send reset link'));
          await tester.tap(find.text('Send reset link'));
          await tester.pumpAndSettle();

       7:   final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

      10:   const TokenStorage({this.auth}) : _storage = const FlutterSecureStorage();
      11:
      12:   Future<String?> readAccessToken() async {
      13:     final currentAuth = auth;
      14:     if (currentAuth != null) return currentAuth.currentUser?.getIdToken();
      15:     return _storage.read(key: _accessTokenKey);
      16:   }
      17:
      18:   Future<void> writeAccessToken(String token) =>
      19:       _storage.write(key: _accessTokenKey, value: token);
      20:

      22: }
    ### lib/core/network/api_client.dart
       1: import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
       2:
       3: import '../config/app_config.dart';
       4: import '../storage/token_storage.dart';
       5:
       6: class ApiClient {
       7:   final Dio dio;
       8:
       9:   ApiClient({required TokenStorage tokenStorage, Dio? dio})
      10:     : dio =
      11:           dio ??
      12:           Dio(
      13:             BaseOptions(
      14:               baseUrl: AppConfig.apiBaseUrl,
      15:               connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
      16:               receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
      17:               headers: const {'Accept': 'application/json'},
      18:             ),
      19:           ) {
      20:     this.dio.interceptors.add(
      21:       InterceptorsWrapper(
      22:         onRequest: (options, handler) async {
      23:           try {
      24:             final token = await tokenStorage.readAccessToken();
      25:             if (token != null && token.trim().isNotEmpty) {
      26:               options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer ${token.trim()}';
      27:             }
      28:             handler.next(options);
      29:           } catch (error) {
      30:             handler.reject(DioException(requestOptions: options, error: error));
      31:           }
      32:         },
      33:       ),
      34:     );
      35:   }
      36: }

       9:
      10: class RecoveryFailure implements Exception {
      11:   const RecoveryFailure(
      12:     this.message, {
      13:     this.invalidLink = false,
      14:     this.passwordUpdated = false,
      15:   });
      16:
      17:   final String message;
      18:   final bool invalidLink;
      19:   final bool passwordUpdated;
      20: }
      21:
      22: class PasswordRecoveryService {
      23:   PasswordRecoveryService(this.auth);
      24:
      25:   final FirebaseAuth auth;
      26:
      27:   static const requestConfirmation =
      28:       'If an account uses this email, you will receive a password reset link. '
      29:       'Check your inbox and spam folder.';
      30:
      31:   static String? emailError(String? value) {
      32:     final email = value?.trim() ?? '';
      33:     if (email.isEmpty) return 'Enter your registered email address.';
      34:     if (email.length > 254 ||
      35:         !RegExp(r'^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$').hasMatch(email)) {
      36:       return 'Enter a valid email address.';
      37:     }
      38:     return null;
      39:   }
      40:
      41:   // Accept an email action link or its code; Firebase remains the authority
      42:   // for the code's project, expiry and single-use validity.
      43:   static String actionCode(String value) {
      44:     final input = value.trim();
      45:     final uri = Uri.tryParse(input);
      46:     String? code;
      47:     if (uri != null && uri.hasScheme) {
      48:       final mode = uri.queryParametersAll['mode'];
      49:       final codes = uri.queryParametersAll['oobCode'];
      50:       if ((uri.scheme != 'https' && uri.scheme != 'fitnep') ||
      51:           mode?.length != 1 ||
      52:           mode!.single != 'resetPassword' ||
      53:           codes?.length != 1) {
      54:         throw const RecoveryFailure(
      55:           'Use the password reset link from your email.',
      56:           invalidLink: true,
      57:         );
      58:       }
      59:       code = codes!.single;
      60:     } else {
      61:       code = input;
      62:     }
      63:     if (!RegExp(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{6,2048}$').hasMatch(code)) {
      64:       throw const RecoveryFailure(
      65:         'This reset link is invalid. Request a new link.',
      66:         invalidLink: true,
      67:       );
      68:     }
      69:     return code;
      70:   }
      71:
      72:   Future<void> request(String email) async {
      73:     final error = emailError(email);
      74:     if (error != null) throw RecoveryFailure(error);
      75:     try {
      76:       await auth.sendPasswordResetEmail(email: email.trim());
      77:     } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
      78:       // Older projects can still return these without enumeration protection.
      79:       if (error.code == 'user-not-found' || error.code == 'user-disabled') {
      80:         return;
      81:       }
      82:       throw failureFor(error);
      83:     }
      84:   }
      85:
      86:   Future<String> verify(String code) async {
      87:     try {
      88:       return await auth.verifyPasswordResetCode(actionCode(code));
      89:     } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
      90:       throw failureFor(error);
      91:     }
      92:   }
      93:
      94:   Future<void> confirm(String code, String password) async {
      95:     if (password.isEmpty) {
      96:       throw const RecoveryFailure('Enter your new password.');
      97:     }
      98:     try {
      99:       final status = await auth.validatePassword(auth, password);
     100:       if (!status.isValid) {
     101:         final missing = <String>[
     102:           if (!status.meetsMinPasswordLength) 'more characters',
     103:           if (!status.meetsMaxPasswordLength) 'fewer characters',
     104:           if (!status.meetsLowercaseRequirement) 'a lowercase letter',
     105:           if (!status.meetsUppercaseRequirement) 'an uppercase letter',
     106:           if (!status.meetsDigitsRequirement) 'a number',
     107:           if (!status.meetsSymbolsRequirement) 'a special character',
     108:         ];
     109:         throw RecoveryFailure(
     110:           missing.isEmpty
     111:               ? 'Your password does not meet the account security requirements.'
     112:               : 'Your password needs ${missing.join(', ')}.',
     113:         );
     114:       }
     115:       await auth.confirmPasswordReset(
     116:         code: actionCode(code),
     117:         newPassword: password,
     118:       );
     119:     } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
     120:       throw failureFor(error);
     121:     } on RecoveryFailure {
     122:       rethrow;
     123:     } catch (_) {
     124:       throw const RecoveryFailure(
     125:         'We couldnâ€™t check your password requirements. Check your connection '
     126:         'and try again.',
     127:       );
     128:     }
     129:     await clearLocalSession();
     130:   }
     131:
     132:   Future<void> clearLocalSession() async {
     133:     try {
     134:       await auth.signOut();
     135:     } catch (_) {
     136:       // The reset code has already been consumed. Retry sign-out, not reset.
     137:       throw const RecoveryFailure(
     138:         'Your password was updated, but we couldnâ€™t sign out this device. '
     139:         'Please finish signing out before continuing.',
     140:         passwordUpdated: true,
     141:       );
     142:     }
     143:   }
     144:
     145:   // Call from the shared session lifecycle on resume/refresh. This reports
     146:   // whether an old session ended; it is not authorization for protected data.
     147:   Future<bool> signOutIfSessionRevoked() async {
     148:     final user = auth.currentUser;
     149:     if (user == null) return false;
     150:     final refreshToken = user.refreshToken;
     151:     try {
     152:       await user.getIdToken(true);
     153:       return false;
     154:     } on FirebaseAuthException catch (error) {
     155:       if (!const {
     156:         'user-token-expired',
     157:         'invalid-user-token',
     158:         'user-disabled',
     159:         'user-not-found',
     160:       }.contains(error.code)) {
     161:         rethrow;
     162:       }
     163:       // Preserve a fresh sign-in made while this older session was checked.
     164:       final current = auth.currentUser;
     165:       if (current?.uid == user.uid && current?.refreshToken == refreshToken) {
     166:         await auth.signOut();
     167:       }
     168:       return true;
     169:     }
     170:   }
     171:
     172:   static RecoveryFailure failureFor(FirebaseAuthException error) {
     173:     return switch (error.code) {
     174:       'expired-action-code' ||
     175:       'invalid-action-code' ||
     176:       'user-disabled' ||
     177:       'user-not-found' => const RecoveryFailure(
     178:         'This reset link has expired or was already used. Request a new link.',
     179:         invalidLink: true,
     180:       ),
     181:       'weak-password' ||
     182:       'password-does-not-meet-requirements' => const RecoveryFailure(
     183:         'Choose a stronger password that meets your account requirements.',
     184:       ),
     185:       'too-many-requests' => const RecoveryFailure(
     186:         'Too many attempts. Please wait a while before trying again.',
     187:       ),
     188:       'network-request-failed' => const RecoveryFailure(
     189:         'Check your connection and try again.',
     190:       ),
     191:       'invalid-email' => const RecoveryFailure('Enter a valid email address.'),
     192:       _ => const RecoveryFailure(
     193:         'Password recovery is unavailable right now. Please try again later.',
     194:       ),
     195:     };
     196:   }
     197: }

      23:
      24: class _PasswordResetSessionObserverState
      25:     extends ConsumerState<PasswordResetSessionObserver>
      26:     with WidgetsBindingObserver {
      27:   late final PasswordRecoveryService _recovery;

      33:   void initState() {

      44:
      45:   void _startChecking() {
      46:     _timer?.cancel();
      47:     final state = WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState;
      48:     if (!mounted ||
      49:         _recovery.auth.currentUser == null ||
      50:         (state != null && state != AppLifecycleState.resumed)) {
      51:       return;
      52:     }
      53:     _timer = Timer.periodic(
      54:       const Duration(seconds: 30),
      55:       (_) => unawaited(_check()),
      56:     );
      57:     unawaited(_check());
      58:   }
      59:
      60:   Future<void> _check() async {
      61:     if (_checking || !mounted) return;
      62:     _checking = true;
      63:     try {
      64:       final ended = await _recovery.signOutIfSessionRevoked();
      65:       if (mounted && ended && _recovery.auth.currentUser == null) {
      66:         _timer?.cancel();

      69:         // Recovery stays usable while signed out; preserve reset confirmation.

      75:     } catch (_) {
      76:       // Connection failures do not prove revocation. Retry on resume/next tick;
      77:       // protected server operations still require revocation-aware verification.
      78:     } finally {
      79:       _checking = false;
      80:     }
      81:   }
      82:
      83:   @override

      85:     if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {

      87:     } else {
      88:       _timer?.cancel();
      89:     }
      90:   }
      91:

    ### README.md
       1: # FitNEP
       2:
       3: Flutter nutrition and fitness app for Android and iOS.
       4:
       5: ## Current implementation
       6:
       7: The app now initializes the shared Firebase development project and opens a
       8: FitNEP welcome screen with navigation. Startup has loading and retry states.
       9: Riverpod provides shared Firebase Auth, Firestore and Functions dependencies.
      10:
      11: US 2 password recovery now has email requests, reset-link/code verification,
      12: new-password forms, Firebase password-policy checks and local sign-out. Revoked
      13: sessions are also checked on app resume and every 30 seconds in the foreground. The
      14: recovery screens follow the supplied auth screenshots. Login/registration (US 1)
      15: belong to the authentication teammate; package selection and the remaining flows
      16: are still to be implemented. The original logo export remains pending.
      17:
      18: See [password recovery integration](docs/password-recovery.md) for route names,
      19: Firebase setup, session enforcement and verification.
      20:
      21: Dilochan's contribution adds the admin client list/profile/audit screens,
      22: client home, notifications and routes to related diet plans, conversations,
      23: tasks and payments. Admin data currently comes from the local mock datasource.
      24: The shared router starts at welcome and retains all recovery and feature routes;
      25: US 1 must connect authenticated navigation and trusted role claims.
      26:
      27: The notification API base URL defaults to empty because this Spark project has
      28: no deployed notification backend. The screens show a configuration error until
      29: a backend is explicitly supplied:
      30:
      31: ```powershell
      32: flutter run -d <android-device-id> --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://api.example.com/
      33: ```
      34:
      35: Notifications use `GET /notifications`, `PATCH /notifications/{id}/read` and
      36: `PATCH /notifications/read-all`. Requests read the current shared Firebase ID
      37: token, and the server derives recipient identity from that verified token.
      38: Sign-out clears cached notifications and destination records. Diet-plan,
      39: conversation, task and payment record endpoints remain feature integrations;
      40: the notification backend does not implement those record APIs yet.
      41:
      42: The TypeScript notification backend and recovery callable are combined in
      43: `functions/`; both use revocation-aware Admin SDK verification. They are retained
      44: for local emulator testing. See [backend setup](functions/README.md) for API
      45: schemas, custom claims, workflow hooks, indexes and tests. No deployment or
      46: billing change is part of this merge.
      47:
      48: ## Workspace and tools
      49:
      50: Repository: `D:\fitnep\fitnep`.
      51: Installed SDK: Flutter 3.47.5 / Dart 3.13.4 at `D:\flutter`.
      52: Use a compatible SDK matching `pubspec.yaml`.
      53:
      54: ```powershell
      55: flutter pub get
      56: flutter devices
      57: flutter run -d <android-device-id>
      58: ```
      59:
      60: iOS builds and device validation require macOS and Xcode.
      61: Firebase client configuration currently supports Android and iOS.
      62:
      63: ## Shared Firebase development project
      64:
      65: Project ID: `fitnep-88908`.
      66: Console: https://console.firebase.google.com/project/fitnep-88908/overview
      67:
      68: | Platform | Identifier | Firebase app ID |
      69: | --- | --- | --- |
      70: | Android | com.example.fitnep | 1:1005066655980:android:439e90e7ff1e252483edc9 |
      71: | iOS Runner | com.example.fitnep | 1:1005066655980:ios:b66d19dd781a157b83edc9 |
      72:
      73: Client configuration is in:
      74: - `lib/firebase_options.dart`
      75: - `android/app/google-services.json`
      76: - `ios/Runner/GoogleService-Info.plist`
      77:
      78: These files identify the client applications and are versioned for reproducible
      79: team setup. They are not server credentials. Keep private service-account keys,
      80: signing credentials and local secrets out of the mobile app and Git history.
      81:
      82: The Android Google Services plugin and iOS Runner plist resource are configured.
      83: Kotlin incremental compilation is disabled to support a Windows workspace and
      84: Pub cache on different drives.
      85: Android builds also limit Gradle workers and compile Kotlin in the Gradle JVM
      86: to keep memory use suitable for development laptops.
      87:
      88: For the first full Android build on a memory-constrained Windows machine, the
      89: following runtime settings can reduce native compiler pressure. They apply only
      90: to the current shell/build and are restored afterwards:
      91:
      92: ```powershell
      93: $previousFitNepJavaOptions = $env:JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
      94: try {
      95:   $env:JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS = "$previousFitNepJavaOptions -XX:ActiveProcessorCount=2 -XX:HeapBaseMinAddress=8G".Trim()
      96:   flutter build apk --debug
      97: } finally {
      98:   $env:JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS = $previousFitNepJavaOptions
      99: }
     100: ```
     101: Firestore databases, authorization rules, Functions deployment and enabled Auth
     102: providers are separate server setup tasks; app registration does not create them.
     103:
     104: Invite teammates to the Firebase project through their own Google accounts.
     105: Email/Password, enumeration protection and the development password policy are
     106: enabled. The project will remain on Spark. Password recovery uses Firebase Auth
     107: directly; the locally tested Functions example is optional and is not deployed.
     108: Agree the shared Firestore schema, region and server rules before implementing
     109: package operations.
     110:
     111: ## Shared code
     112:
     113: - `lib/app/app.dart`: app setup and startup boundary.
     114: - `lib/app/routes/`: named routes and router lifecycle.
     115: - `lib/app/theme/`: provisional light/dark theme.
     116: - `lib/core/services/firebase_providers.dart`: initialization and injectable SDK dependencies.
     117: - `lib/shared/widgets/`: page layout, startup feedback and text wordmark.
     118: - `lib/features/`: existing feature folders; use the files needed for each story.
     119:
     120: Read Firebase SDK providers after initialization completes. Override the providers
     121: in feature tests instead of connecting widget tests to the live project.
     122:
     123: The recovery callable uses `us-central1`; the notification API and workflow hooks
     124: use `asia-south1`. The shared callable SDK defaults to `us-central1` and can be
     125: configured for a different callable region:
     126:
     127: ```powershell
     128: flutter run -d <android-device-id> --dart-define=FIREBASE_FUNCTIONS_REGION=<deployed-region>
     129: ```
     130:
     131: ## Checks and cooperation
     132:
     133: ```powershell
     134: dart format --output=none --set-exit-if-changed lib test integration_test
     135: flutter analyze
     136: flutter test --concurrency=1
     137: flutter build apk --debug
     138: git diff --check
     139: ```
     140:
     141: GitHub Actions runs format, analysis and widget tests for the shared branches.
     142: The widget tests check startup gating, recovery navigation, form validation,
     143: retry, reset completion and keyboard use with larger text on a small display.
     144: Functions unit/emulator tests cover reset-code replay, changed credentials and
     145: revoked-session access. Live Android recovery, mailbox delivery, actual provider
     146: expiry, equivalent public email responses and rejection of old credentials and
     147: multiple session tokens were verified on 18 November 2026. The login handoff and
     148: iOS checks remain pending. Protected-data features need authorization and
     149: revocation rules designed for Spark before integration; see the recovery
     150: document for the evidence and its limits.
     151:
     152: Use personal work branches and PR into `development`. Include story IDs,
     153: acceptance evidence, tests and any shared Firebase/schema changes in the PR.
     154: Use the agreed release branch and GitHub Releases for tested APK prereleases;
     155: do not commit build output.
     156:
     157: The four-day work plan, Firebase teammate handoff and release-notes template are
     158: maintained separately in `D:\fitnep_plan`.
     159:
     160: The Prajwol/Dilochan integration passed 70 Flutter tests, 18 backend unit tests,
     161: 21 combined Firebase emulator checks, formatting, the analyzer and the Android
     162: debug APK build. Local backend checks used Node 24; the declared runtime and CI
     163: use Node 22. These checks did not deploy Functions or change Spark billing.
    ### functions/README.md
       1: # FitNep Firebase notification backend
       2:
       3: This folder contains the mobile application's Firebase Cloud Functions backend.
       4: It exposes authenticated HTTPS APIs for the Flutter app; it does not contain a
       5: website or browser UI.
       6:
       7: The shared project stays on **Spark**. These Functions are local emulator
       8: implementations; they are not deployed. Cloud Functions deployment requires a
       9: billing-enabled project and is outside the agreed scope. The Flutter API URL
      10: defaults to empty until a supported backend is supplied.
      11:
      12: ## Firebase project and runtime
      13:
      14: - Project: `fitnep-88908`
      15: - Region: `asia-south1`
      16: - Runtime: Node.js 22
      17: - Exported HTTPS function: `api`
      18: - Recovery callable: `checkSession`, in `us-central1`
      19: - Shared entry point: `lib/index.js`, built from TypeScript; it also exports
      20:   the existing CommonJS recovery callable from `src/index.cjs`.
      21:
      22: For local emulators, configure an Android emulator with:
      23:
      24: ```powershell
      25: flutter run --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=http://10.0.2.2:5001/demo-fitnep/asia-south1/api/
      26: ```
      27:
      28: ## Authentication and roles
      29:
      30: Every API request must include a Firebase ID token:
      31:
      32: ```text
      33: Authorization: Bearer <firebase-id-token>
      34: ```
      35:
      36: The token must have one of these trusted custom claims:
      37:
      38: ```json
      39: { "role": "admin" }
      40: { "role": "dietitian" }
      41: { "role": "client" }
      42: ```
      43:
      44: Role claims must be assigned by a trusted administrative process using the
      45: Firebase Admin SDK. Never allow the mobile app to assign or edit its own role.
      46:
      47: All roles are scoped to their own `uid` for notification access. Admin and
      48: Dietitian roles do not receive an implicit bypass to client notifications.
      49:
      50: ## Notification APIs
      51:
      52: - `GET /notifications`
      53: - `GET /notifications/:notificationId`
      54: - `PATCH /notifications/:notificationId/read`
      55: - `PATCH /notifications/read-all`
      56:
      57: Recipient identity is always derived from the verified token. Requests that
      58: try to supply `recipient_id`, `recipientId`, `user_id`, or `userId` are rejected.
      59: Unknown and unauthorized notification IDs both return `404` to avoid leaking
      60: whether another user's notification exists.
      61:
      62: ## Workflow hooks
      63:
      64: Cloud Functions listen to backend-owned Firestore workflow collections for:
      65:
      66: - `diet_plans/{planId}` creation;
      67: - `messages/{messageId}` creation;
      68: - `tasks/{taskId}` creation;
      69: - `payments/{paymentId}` transitions into `paid`, `confirmed`, or `succeeded`.
      70:
      71: Each hook derives a stable event key so retries do not create duplicates.
      72: Pending and failed payments are ignored. These hooks are backend-only and are
      73: not exposed as public HTTP routes. Workflow documents accept the existing
      74: camel-case names as aliases, but the preferred fields are:
      75:
      76: | Workflow | Required notification fields |
      77: |---|---|
      78: | Diet plan | `client_id`, `assigned_by_id`, `assigned_by_role`; optional `title` |
      79: | Message | `conversation_id`, `recipient_id`; optional `sender_name` |
      80: | Task | `assignee_id`, `assigned_by_id`, `assigned_by_role`; optional `title` |
      81: | Payment | `recipient_id`, `status`; optional `amount_label` |
      82:
      83: Only trusted backend code may write these collections. The included Firestore
      84: rules reject direct mobile reads and writes.
      85:
      86: ## Firestore storage
      87:
      88: Production uses `FirestoreNotificationRepository`. Notification documents are
      89: stored in `notifications` with this schema:
      90:
      91: - `id` (Firestore document ID)
      92: - `recipient_id`
      93: - `title`
      94: - `message`
      95: - `type`
      96: - `reference_id`
      97: - `is_read`
      98: - `created_at`
      99: - `event_key` (internal idempotency key)
     100:
     101: Every query and mutation includes the authenticated user's UID. Deterministic
     102: SHA-256 document IDs and Firestore transactions make workflow retries
     103: idempotent. Composite indexes are declared in `../firestore.indexes.json`.
     104:
     105: ## Commands
     106:
     107: ```powershell
     108: cd functions
     109: npm ci
     110: npm run build
     111: npm test
     112: ```
     113:
     114: The declared Node 22 runtime uses Firebase Admin 14 and pins its database-compat
     115: dependency to the previously tested Node-compatible release. Keep the lockfile
     116: when installing shared dependencies.
     117:
     118: Run the combined recovery, notification and workflow security tests from the
     119: repository root. These tests use only `demo-fitnep`, never the live project:
     120:
     121: ```powershell
     122: $env:JAVA_HOME = 'D:\studio\jbr'
     123: $env:PATH = "$env:JAVA_HOME\bin;$env:PATH"
     124: $env:FITNEP_RUN_TRIGGER_TESTS = 'true'
     125: npm --prefix functions run build
     126: firebase emulators:exec --only auth,functions,firestore --project demo-fitnep --non-interactive "npm --prefix functions run test:emulator"
     127: ```
     128:
     129: Run all four workflow-trigger integration tests with the Functions emulator:
     130:
     131: ```powershell
     132: $env:FITNEP_RUN_TRIGGER_TESTS = 'true'
     133: npm --prefix functions run build
     134: npm --prefix functions run build:test
     135: npx --yes firebase-tools@latest emulators:exec --config firebase.json --project demo-fitnep --only firestore,functions "node --test --test-isolation=none --test-concurrency=1 functions/.test-build/test/workflow_trigger_emulator.test.js"
     136: ```
     137:
     138: Firestore uses port 18080 in the shared configuration. The emulator-only
     139: commands work without deploying to the Spark project.
     140:
     141: The retained deployment script is outside the supported Spark workflow. No
     142: Functions deployment or billing upgrade is part of this integration. A live
     143: notification service needs a separately agreed hosting arrangement.
