    import 'dart:async';
    
    import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/features/authentication/data/authentication_service.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
    
    import '../support/memory_session_preference_store.dart';
    
    class _User extends Fake implements User {
      @override
      String get uid => 'client-1';
    }
    
    class _Credential extends Fake implements UserCredential {}
    
    class _Auth extends Fake implements FirebaseAuth {
      User? user;
      FirebaseAuthException? signInError;
      String? email;
      String? password;
      int signIns = 0;
      int signOuts = 0;
      final changes = StreamController<User?>.broadcast();
    
      @override
      User? get currentUser => user;
    
      @override
      Stream<User?> authStateChanges() async* {
        yield user;
        yield* changes.stream;
      }
    
      @override
      Future<UserCredential> signInWithEmailAndPassword({
        required String email,
        required String password,
      }) async {
        signIns++;
        this.email = email;
        this.password = password;
        if (signInError != null) throw signInError!;
        user = _User();
        changes.add(user);
        return _Credential();
      }
    
      @override
      Future<void> signOut() async {
        signOuts++;
        user = null;
        changes.add(null);
      }
    }
    
    void main() {
      late _Auth auth;
      late MemorySessionPreferenceStore preferences;
      late AuthenticationService service;
    
      setUp(() {
        auth = _Auth();
        preferences = MemorySessionPreferenceStore();
        service = AuthenticationService(
          auth: auth,
          sessionPreferences: preferences,
        );
      });
    
      tearDown(() async {
        await auth.changes.close();
      });
    
      test('sign in uses Firebase and stores only the remember preference', () async {
        await service.signIn(
          email: ' client@example.com ',
          password: ' Password!12 ',
          rememberSession: true,
        );
    
        expect(auth.signIns, 1);
        expect(auth.email, 'client@example.com');
        expect(auth.password, ' Password!12 ');
        expect(preferences.rememberSession, isTrue);
        expect(preferences.writes, 1);
      });
    
      test('invalid credentials use a safe public error', () async {
        auth.signInError = FirebaseAuthException(code: 'invalid-credential');
    
        await expectLater(
          service.signIn(
            email: 'client@example.com',
            password: 'wrong',
            rememberSession: false,
          ),
          throwsA(
            isA<AuthenticationFailure>()
                .having((error) => error.code, 'code', 'invalid-credential')
                .having(
                  (error) => error.message,
                  'message',
                  'The email or password is incorrect.',
                ),
          ),
        );
      });
    
      test('restore signs out an explicitly unremembered session', () async {
        auth.user = _User();
        preferences.rememberSession = false;
    
        await service.restoreSession();
    
        expect(auth.signOuts, 1);
        expect(auth.currentUser, isNull);
      });
    
      test('restore preserves remembered and pre-policy sessions', () async {
        auth.user = _User();
        preferences.rememberSession = true;
        await service.restoreSession();
        expect(auth.signOuts, 0);
    
        preferences.rememberSession = null;
        await service.restoreSession();
        expect(auth.signOuts, 0);
      });
    
      test('logout clears Firebase state and the remember preference', () async {
        auth.user = _User();
        preferences.rememberSession = true;
    
        await service.signOut();
    
        expect(auth.signOuts, 1);
        expect(auth.currentUser, isNull);
        expect(preferences.rememberSession, isNull);
        expect(preferences.clears, 1);
      });
    }
    Add: C:\Projects\fitnep\test\support\memory_session_preference_store.dart
    import 'package:fitnep/features/authentication/data/session_preference_store.dart';
    
    class MemorySessionPreferenceStore implements SessionPreferenceStore {
      MemorySessionPreferenceStore({this.rememberSession});
    
      bool? rememberSession;
      int writes = 0;
      int clears = 0;
    
      @override
      Future<bool?> readRememberSession() async => rememberSession;
    
      @override
      Future<void> writeRememberSession(bool rememberSession) async {
        writes++;
        this.rememberSession = rememberSession;
      }
    
      @override
      Future<void> clearRememberSession() async {
        clears++;
        rememberSession = null;
      }
    }

    import 'dart:async';
    
    import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/app.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/routes/app_router.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/app/routes/app_routes.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/core/services/firebase_providers.dart';
    import 'package:fitnep/features/authentication/presentation/controllers/authentication_controller.dart';
    import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
    import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
    
    import '../support/memory_session_preference_store.dart';
    
    class _User extends Fake implements User {
      @override
      String get uid => 'client-1';
    }
    
    class _Credential extends Fake implements UserCredential {}
    
    class _Auth extends Fake implements FirebaseAuth {
      _Auth({this.user});
    
      User? user;
      FirebaseAuthException? signInError;
      String? email;
      String? password;
      int signOuts = 0;
      final changes = StreamController<User?>.broadcast();
    
      @override
      User? get currentUser => user;
    
      @override
      Stream<User?> authStateChanges() async* {
        yield user;
        yield* changes.stream;
      }
    
      @override
      Future<UserCredential> signInWithEmailAndPassword({
        required String email,
        required String password,
      }) async {
        this.email = email;
        this.password = password;
        if (signInError != null) throw signInError!;
        user = _User();
        changes.add(user);
        return _Credential();
      }
    
      @override
      Future<void> signOut() async {
        signOuts++;
        user = null;
        changes.add(null);
      }
    }
    
    Future<ProviderContainer> _open(
      WidgetTester tester,
      _Auth auth,
      MemorySessionPreferenceStore preferences,
    ) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(
        ProviderScope(
          overrides: [
            firebaseInitializationProvider.overrideWith((ref) async {}),
            firebaseAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(auth),
            sessionPreferenceStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(preferences),
          ],
          child: const FitNepApp(),
        ),
      );
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      return ProviderScope.containerOf(
        tester.element(find.byType(FitNepApp)),
      );
    }
    
    Future<void> _close(WidgetTester tester, _Auth auth) async {
      await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox.shrink());
      await tester.pump();
      await auth.changes.close();
    }
    
    void main() {
      testWidgets('signed-out users are redirected away from protected routes', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final auth = _Auth();
        final container = await _open(
          tester,
          auth,
          MemorySessionPreferenceStore(),
        );
    
        container.read(appRouterProvider).go(AppRoutes.clientHome);
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    
        expect(find.text('Welcome back'), findsOneWidget);
        expect(find.text('Client Home'), findsNothing);
        await _close(tester, auth);
      });
    
      testWidgets('real Firebase login reaches development screen and logs out', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final auth = _Auth();
        final preferences = MemorySessionPreferenceStore();
        await _open(tester, auth, preferences);
    
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Log in'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        await tester.enterText(
          find.byType(TextFormField).at(0),
          ' client@example.com ',
        );
        await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField).at(1), 'Password!12');
        await tester.tap(find.byType(Checkbox));
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Log in'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    
        expect(auth.email, 'client@example.com');
        expect(auth.password, 'Password!12');
        expect(preferences.rememberSession, isTrue);
        expect(find.text('App Under Development'), findsOneWidget);
        expect(find.text('Coming Soon'), findsOneWidget);
    
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Logout'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    
        expect(auth.signOuts, 1);
        expect(preferences.rememberSession, isNull);
        expect(find.text('Welcome back'), findsOneWidget);
        await _close(tester, auth);
      });
    
      testWidgets('login failures are shown without exposing Firebase details', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final auth = _Auth()
          ..signInError = FirebaseAuthException(code: 'invalid-credential');
        await _open(tester, auth, MemorySessionPreferenceStore());
    
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Log in'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
        await tester.enterText(
          find.byType(TextFormField).at(0),
          'client@example.com',
        );
        await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextFormField).at(1), 'wrong');
        await tester.tap(find.widgetWithText(FilledButton, 'Log in'));
        await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    
        expect(find.text('The email or password is incorrect.'), findsOneWidget);
        expect(
          tester
              .widget<TextFormField>(find.byType(TextFormField).at(1))
              .controller
              ?.text,
          isEmpty,
        );
        await _close(tester, auth);
      });
    
      testWidgets('remembered sessions open the development screen on startup', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final auth = _Auth(user: _User());
        await _open(
          tester,
          auth,
          MemorySessionPreferenceStore(rememberSession: true),
        );
    
        expect(find.text('App Under Development'), findsOneWidget);
        expect(auth.signOuts, 0);
        await _close(tester, auth);
      });
    
      testWidgets('unremembered sessions sign out before protected UI appears', (
        tester,
      ) async {
        final auth = _Auth(user: _User());
        await _open(
          tester,
          auth,
          MemorySessionPreferenceStore(rememberSession: false),
        );
    
        expect(find.text('App Under Development'), findsNothing);
        expect(find.text('Learn about FitNEP'), findsOneWidget);
        expect(auth.signOuts, 1);
        await _close(tester, auth);
      });
    }

## Assistant

Implemented Firebase authentication and protected navigation while preserving existing work.

### Created

- [authentication_service.dart](C:/Projects/fitnep/lib/features/authentication/data/authentication_service.dart:15)
- [session_preference_store.dart](C:/Projects/fitnep/lib/features/authentication/data/session_preference_store.dart:11)
- [authentication_controller.dart](C:/Projects/fitnep/lib/features/authentication/presentation/controllers/authentication_controller.dart:12)
- [app_under_development_page.dart](C:/Projects/fitnep/lib/features/authentication/presentation/pages/app_under_development_page.dart:9)
- Three authentication test/support files.

### Modified

- App startup/auth restoration.
- GoRouter authentication guards and temporary route.
- Existing Login page behavior without redesigning it.
- Password-reset completion and revoked-session navigation.
- Related tests and authentication documentation.

Existing theme, onboarding, admin, and generated-file changes remain preserved.

### Authentication flow

- Real `signInWithEmailAndPassword`.
- Riverpod-managed bootstrap, auth state, login/logout loading, and errors.
- Firebase-native session restoration.
- Remember Me stores only a boolean:
  - Checked: native Firebase session is restored.
  - Unchecked: session is signed out before protected UI appears on the next cold start.
- Existing pre-policy sessions remain authenticated.
- No passwords or Firebase tokens are manually stored.
- Existing `TokenStorage` was inspected and left unchanged; no token-writing APIs were introduced or used.
- All non-public routes require authentication.
- Successful login opens App Under Development.
- Logout calls Firebase `signOut()` and returns to Login.
- Forgot Password and Reset Password remain public.
- Temporary screen includes FITNEP branding, construction icon, Coming Soon message, and Logout.

### Firebase configuration

Email/Password authentication is confirmed enabled. Existing Android/iOS Firebase configuration is used; no further Console changes are required.

No fake Firebase accounts or mock production authentication were created. Automated tests use isolated test doubles only.

### Verification

- `flutter analyze --no-pub`: no issues.
- Focused authentication tests: 10 passed.
- Complete Flutter suite: 80 passed.
- `git diff --check`: passed.
- No new dependencies added.

Remaining validation: a live login was not attempted because no real account credentials were provided; iOS device behavior also remains device-validation work. Generated desktop plugin files still show their pre-existing line-ending-only modified status.
